        let getUtmData = function() {
            let utmData = {};
            let params = window.location.search;
            params = params.substr(1);
            let refparams = params.replace("&amp;", "&");
            let queryParamArray = refparams.split('&');
            queryParamArray.forEach(function(queryParam) {
                let entry = queryParam.split('=');
                if(entry[0].startsWith("utm")){
                    utmData [entry[0]] = entry[1];
                }
            });
            return utmData;
        }
        // The root object for unswAepData
        window.unswAepData = {
            pageinfo: JSON.parse("{\x22siteName\x22:\x22UNSW Sites\x22,\x22timeParting\x22:\x2209 Jul 2025|WEDNESDAY|19:32:59\x22,\x22platformType\x22:\x22AEM\x22,\x22siteCategory\x22:\x22UNSW\x22,\x22buildVersion\x22:\x226.5.21.0\x22,\x22pageName\x22:\x22unsw sites:en:course\u002Doutlines:course\u002Doutline\x22,\x22pageTitle\x22:\x22Course Outline\x22,\x22pageHierarchy\x22:[\x22UNSW\x22,\x22Course Outline Finder\x22,\x22Course Outline\x22],\x22buildDate\x22:\x222024\u002D08\u002D15\x22,\x22language\x22:\x22en\x22,\x22country\x22:\x22au\x22,\x22AEMdesignVersion\x22:\x22AEM\u002D6.5.21.0\x22}"),
            marketinginfo: getUtmData(),
            userinfo: {} // TODO
        };
        let previousName = document.referrer;
        if (window.localStorage) {
            let previousNameLocal = localStorage.getItem('unswAepData-previousName');
            if (previousNameLocal && previousNameLocal !== window.unswAepData.pageinfo.pageName) {
                previousName = previousNameLocal;
            }
            localStorage.setItem('unswAepData-previousName', window.unswAepData.pageinfo.pageName);
        }

        window.unswAepData.pageinfo = Object.assign(window.unswAepData.pageinfo, {
            pageURL: location.origin + location.pathname,
            referringURL: document.referrer,
            previousPage: previousName,
            hostname: location.hostname,
        });

        window.adobeDataLayer = window.adobeDataLayer || [];
        window.adobeDataLayer.push({
            event: "pageview",
            pageinfo:  window.unswAepData.pageinfo,
            marketinginfo: window.unswAepData.marketinginfo,
            userInfo: { ...window.unswAepData.userinfo },
        });
    </script>

        <div role="tabpanel" class="cmp-tabs__tabpanel CourseDetails&amp;OutcomesTab" data-cmp-hook-tabs="tabpanel" id="panel-1"><div class="column-layout uds-component padded-left padded-right">
<span data-component_type="column-layout" data-component_id="item-2029148582" data-component_title="Course Outline">


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12">

    
        <div class="responsivegrid aem-GridColumn--default--none aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 aem-GridColumn--medium--12 aem-GridColumn--small--12 aem-GridColumn--offset--default--0">
            <div class="spacer section">


    

<span data-component_type="spacer" data-component_id="spacer-1786353895" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-spacer__xsmall"></div>
</span>
</div>
<div class="heading-component uds-component section"><span data-component_type="heading" data-component_id="heading-930938705" data-component_title="Course Details &amp; Outcomes">
  <h2 id="heading-930938705" class="left">
      Course Details &amp; Outcomes
  </h2>
</span>


    

</div>
<div class="text uds-component section dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-215017556" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <h3>Course Description</h3>
<div><p>COMP6080 aims to develop your confidence in building modern web-based applications to industry standards. This occurs through the introduction of a range of basic concepts surrounding HTML, CSS, Vanilla Javascript, Javascript Declarative Frameworks, UI/UX Principles, Accessibility, Network &amp; Asynchronous Programming, Front-end Testing, and other basic infrastructure.</p>
<p>This course has a heavy emphasis on industry voices, and as such a number of lectures will be given by current front-end developers from industry. These lectures primarily come from employees at Canva, a Sydney-based technology company that does a lot of work with front-end technologies.</p>
<p>COMP6080 is a challenging course. Front-end development is unlike most things you've experienced at university before. You will find the individual problems you solve much simpler than other level 6 courses, but the time you will feel that you spend on the aggregate of these issues will feel larger. A number of students will find this course quite time consuming if they're hoping to achieve a high mark. We'd encourage you to reflect on this fact before you enrol in the course.</p></div>

</div>

    

</span>


</div>
<div class="text uds-component section dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-174114268" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <h3>Course Aims</h3>
<div><p>COMP6080 aims to develop your confidence in building modern web-based applications to industry standards.</p>
<p>COMP6080 is a higher-level elective course and assumes that you have the following:</p>
<ul>
<li>A high level understanding of how interpreted scripting languages work (e.g. python), in terms of inputs, interpretation, loose typing, etc</li>
<li>Ability to use GIT as a version management tool</li>
<li>Basics of HTTP protocol and interacting with web browsers</li>
</ul>
<p>Postgraduate students may need to familiarise themselves with GIT, if not already familiar.</p></div>

</div>

    

</span>


</div>
<div class="text uds-component section empty-hidden-component dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-1171570569" data-component_title="Course Outline">

      cmp-text--table-padding-

<p></p>

</dd></dl></div></li><li><div class="table-listing-card"><dl class="table-listing-cell"><dt><strong>Course Learning Outcomes</strong></dt><dd>CLO4 : Build stable applications that utilise concurrent programming through use of Javascript's asynchronous programming techniques</dd></dl><dl class="table-listing-cell"><dt><strong>Assessment Item</strong></dt><dd><ul>

</dd></dl></div></li><li><div class="table-listing-card"><dl class="table-listing-cell"><dt><strong>Course Learning Outcomes</strong></dt><dd>CLO5 : Design and build interfaces that focus on best user experience and accessible design practices</dd></dl><dl class="table-listing-cell"><dt><strong>Assessment Item</strong></dt><dd><ul>

    <h5>Assessment Overview</h5>
<div><div>
<div>Students will build a full web application front-end based on a modern framework that works with a provided back-end server.</div>
<div>&nbsp;</div>
<div>Student's work will be marked by manually checking for completed features; reviewing code to look for quality code; ensuring that the finished product is mobile responsive; ensuring that user experience and accessibility is expressed effectively; and ensuring that the application is well tested.</div>
<div>&nbsp;</div>
<div>This criteria will be provided to both students and tutors. Tutors will share marks and comments with students.</div>
<div>&nbsp;</div>
For pair assignments, you complete them a pair of your choice. If you don't have a pair, we will find one for you. You can also choose to work alone, but we strongly do not recommend this as the workload is much higher for an individual.</div>
<div>&nbsp;</div>
<div>Nominations for your pair (or to work alone) must be complete by the end of week 2. Information about this will be distributed in weekly notices.</div>
<div>&nbsp;</div>
<div>Pairs will be&nbsp;<strong>required</strong>&nbsp;to contribute regularly to gitlab and in reasonably equal contributions as we still assess contributions individually (there is no blanket group mark assigned). Failure to do so may result in a loss of marks.</div>
<p>&nbsp;</p></div>

</div>

<div class="accordion uds-component section">




<span data-component_type="accordion" data-component_id="accordion-192415813" data-component_title="Course Outline">

    <section class="accordion">
        <ul>
            
    
    

    <li class="accordion-list-item">
        <button class="accordion-item single-only" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="01952114-a328-4445-8d9f-9997e5774fce" data-click_category="accordion" data-level="1" data-click_id="accordion-2a23511d" data-click_title="Course Outline" data-click_name="Academic Information">
            <div class="accordion-item-heading">
                Academic Information
            </div>
            <span class="accordion-item-button"></span>
        </button>

        <div id="01952114-a328-4445-8d9f-9997e5774fce" class="accordion-content" style="overflow: hidden; height: 461.928px; padding-top: 0px; margin-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 7.03891px; margin-bottom: 0px;">
            


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="text uds-component aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-317335253" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <div><p><b>I. Special consideration and supplementary assessment</b></p>

<p>If you have experienced an illness or misadventure beyond your control that will interfere with your assessment performance, you are eligible to apply for Special Consideration prior to, or within 3 working days of,  submitting an assessment or sitting an exam.</p>

<p>Please note that UNSW has a Fit to Sit / Submit rule, which means that if you sit an exam or submit a piece of assessment, you are declaring yourself fit enough to do so and cannot later apply for Special Consideration.</p>

<p>For details of applying for Special Consideration and conditions for the award of supplementary assessment, please see the information on UNSW’s <a href="https://www.student.unsw.edu.au/special-consideration">Special Consideration page</a>.</p>

<p><b>II. Administrative matters and links</b></p>

<p>All students are expected to read and be familiar with UNSW guidelines and polices. In particular, students should be familiar with the following:</p>

<ul>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/attendance">Attendance</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://www.myit.unsw.edu.au/services/students/email-students-and-staff">UNSW Email Address</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/special-consideration">Special Consideration</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/exams">Exams</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/exam-approved-calculators-and-computers">Approved Calculators</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/plagiarism">Academic Honesty and Plagiarism</a></li>
	<li><a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/els">Equitable Learning Services</a></li>
</ul>

<p><b>III. Equity and diversity</b></p>

<p>Those students who have a disability that requires some adjustment in their teaching or learning environment are encouraged to discuss their study needs with the course convener prior to, or at the commencement of, their course, or with the Equity Officer (Disability) in the Equitable Learning Services. Issues to be discussed may include access to materials, signers or note-takers, the provision of services and additional exam and assessment arrangements. Early notification is essential to enable any necessary adjustments to be made.</p>

<p><i>Note: This course outline sets out the description of classes at the date the Course Outline is published. The nature of classes may change during the Term after the Course Outline is published.  Moodle or your primary learning management system (LMS) should be consulted for the up-to-date class descriptions.  If there is any inconsistency in the description of activities between the University timetable and the Course Outline/Moodle/LMS, the description in the Course Outline/Moodle/LMS applies<b>.</b></i><b> </b></p>
</div>

</div>

    

</span>


</div>

    
</div>

            

        </div>
    </li>

        
            
    
    

    <li class="accordion-list-item">
        <button class="accordion-item single-only" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="f2333999-3583-412a-9424-e2b555194c53" data-click_category="accordion" data-level="1" data-click_id="accordion-1244be8e" data-click_title="Course Outline" data-click_name="Academic Honesty and Plagarism">
            <div class="accordion-item-heading">
                Academic Honesty and Plagarism
            </div>
            <span class="accordion-item-button"></span>
        </button>

        <div id="f2333999-3583-412a-9424-e2b555194c53" class="accordion-content" style="overflow: hidden; height: 227.225px; padding-top: 0px; margin-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 7.03891px; margin-bottom: 0px;">
            


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="text uds-component aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-374593555" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <p><div>
<p>UNSW has an ongoing commitment to fostering a culture of learning informed by academic integrity. All UNSW students have a responsibility to adhere to this principle of academic integrity. Plagiarism undermines academic integrity and is not tolerated at UNSW.&nbsp;<i>Plagiarism at UNSW is defined as using the words or ideas of others and passing them off as your own.</i></p>

<p>Plagiarism is a type of intellectual theft. It can take many forms, from deliberate cheating to accidentally copying from a source without acknowledgement. UNSW has produced a website with a wealth of resources to support students to understand and avoid plagiarism, visit: <a href="https://student.unsw.edu.au/plagiarism">student.unsw.edu.au/plagiarism</a>. The Learning Centre assists students with understanding academic integrity and how not to plagiarise. They also hold workshops and can help students one-on-one.</p>

<p>You are also reminded that careful time management is an important part of study and one of the identified causes of plagiarism is poor time management. Students should allow sufficient time for research, drafting and the proper referencing of sources in preparing all assessment tasks.</p>

<p>Repeated plagiarism (even in first year), plagiarism after first year, or serious instances, may also be investigated under the Student Misconduct Procedures. The penalties under the procedures can include a reduction in marks, failing a course or for the most serious matters (like plagiarism in an honours thesis or contract cheating) even suspension from the university. The Student Misconduct Procedures are available here:</p>

<p><a href="http://www.gs.unsw.edu.au/policy/documents/studentmisconductprocedures.pdf">www.gs.unsw.edu.au/policy/documents/studentmisconductprocedures.pdf</a></p>
</div>
</p>

</div>

    

</span>


</div>

    
</div>

            

        </div>
    </li>

        
            
    
    

    <li class="accordion-list-item">
        <button class="accordion-item single-only" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="69c7cb11-8b5c-4991-a1aa-259aebcadd2d" data-click_category="accordion" data-level="1" data-click_id="accordion-f8893921" data-click_title="Course Outline" data-click_name="Submission of Assessment Tasks">
            <div class="accordion-item-heading">
                Submission of Assessment Tasks
            </div>
            <span class="accordion-item-button"></span>
        </button>

        <div id="69c7cb11-8b5c-4991-a1aa-259aebcadd2d" class="accordion-content" style="overflow: hidden; height: 239.323px; padding-top: 0px; margin-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 7.03891px; margin-bottom: 0px;">
            


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="text uds-component aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-345964404" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <p><div>
<p>Work submitted late without an approved extension by the course coordinator or delegated authority is subject to a late penalty of five percent (5%) for that assessment item.</p>

<p>The late penalty is applied per calendar day (including weekends and public holidays) that the assessment is overdue. There is no pro-rata of the late penalty for submissions made part way through a day. This is for all assessments where a penalty applies.</p>

<p>Work submitted after five days (120 hours) will not be accepted and a mark of zero will be awarded for that assessment item.</p>

<p>For some assessment items, a late penalty may not be appropriate. These will be clearly indicated in the course outline, and such assessments will receive a mark of zero if not completed by the specified date. Examples include:</p>

<ul>
	<li>Weekly online tests or laboratory work worth a small proportion of the subject mark;</li>
	<li>Exams, peer feedback and team evaluation surveys;</li>
	<li>Online quizzes where answers are released to students on completion;</li>
	<li>Professional assessment tasks, where the intention is to create an authentic assessment that has an absolute submission date; and,</li>
	<li>Pass/Fail assessment tasks.</li>
</ul>
</div>
</p>

</div>

    

</span>


</div>

    
</div>

            

        </div>
    </li>

        
            
    
    

    <li class="accordion-list-item">
        <button class="accordion-item single-only" aria-expanded="false" aria-controls="3b5b31bf-204e-48e6-be21-c6ef3560dfc7" data-click_category="accordion" data-level="1" data-click_id="accordion-3a64201b" data-click_title="Course Outline" data-click_name="Faculty-specific Information">
            <div class="accordion-item-heading">
                Faculty-specific Information
            </div>
            <span class="accordion-item-button"></span>
        </button>

        <div id="3b5b31bf-204e-48e6-be21-c6ef3560dfc7" class="accordion-content" style="overflow: hidden; height: 158.375px; padding-top: 0px; margin-top: 0px; padding-bottom: 7.03891px; margin-bottom: 0px;">
            


<div class="aem-Grid aem-Grid--12 aem-Grid--default--12 ">
    
    <div class="text uds-component aem-GridColumn aem-GridColumn--default--12 dynamic-course-content--loaded">



    
<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-403222706" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-
      
      cmp-text--table-alt-color-
      cmp-text--table-header-text-size-
      
      
      
    ">
    <div><p><a href="https://nucleus.unsw.edu.au/en/contact-us" target="_blank">Engineering Student Support Services<span class="sr-only">, opens in a new window</span><i class="fa fa-external-link-alt" style="margin: 0 0.5rem;"></i></a> – The Nucleus - enrolment, progression checks, clash requests, course issues or program-related queries </p>

<p><a href="mailto:eng.wil@unsw.edu.au" target="_blank">Engineering Industrial Training<span class="sr-only">, opens in a new window</span><i class="fa fa-external-link-alt" style="margin: 0 0.5rem;"></i></a> – Industrial training questions </p>

<p><a href="mailto:studyabroad@unsw.edu.au" target="_blank">UNSW Study Abroad<span class="sr-only">, opens in a new window</span><i class="fa fa-external-link-alt" style="margin: 0 0.5rem;"></i></a> – study abroad student enquiries (for inbound students) </p>

<p><a href="mailto:exchange@unsw.edu.au" target="_blank">UNSW Exchange<span class="sr-only">, opens in a new window</span><i class="fa fa-external-link-alt" style="margin: 0 0.5rem;"></i></a> – student exchange enquiries (for inbound students) </p>

<p><a href="https://www.futurestudents.unsw.edu.au/ask-question" target="_blank">UNSW Future Students<span class="sr-only">, opens in a new window</span><i class="fa fa-external-link-alt" style="margin: 0 0.5rem;"></i></a> – potential student enquiries e.g. admissions, fees, programs, credit transfer </p>

<p><b>Phone </b></p>

<p>(+61 2) 9385 8500 – Nucleus Student Hub </p>

                School Contact Information

<span data-component_type="text" data-component_id="text-260076951" data-component_title="Course Outline">
<div class="cmp-text 
      
      
      cmp-text--table-padding-

    ">
    <div><p><b><span><span><span><span><span>CSE Help! - on the Ground Floor of K17</span></span></span></span></span></b></p>

<ul>
	<li><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>For assistance with coursework assessments. </span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></li>
</ul>

<p><b>The Nucleus Student Hub </b>- <span><span><span><a href="https://nucleus.unsw.edu.au/en/contact-us">https://nucleus.unsw.edu.au/en/contact-us</a></span></span></span></p>

<ul>
	<li><span><span><span><span><span>Course enrolment queries.</span></span></span></span></span></li>
</ul>

<p><b>Grievance Officer </b>- <span><span><span><a href="mailto:grievance-officer@cse.unsw.edu.au">grievance-officer@cse.unsw.edu.au</a></span></span></span></p>

<ul>
	<li>If the course <span><span><span>convenor gives an inadequate response to a query or when the courses convenor does not respond to a query about assessment. </span></span></span></li>
</ul>

<p><b>Student Reps </b>-  <span><span><span><a href="mailto:stureps@cse.unsw.edu.au">stureps@cse.unsw.edu.au</a></span></span></span></p>

<ul>
	<li>
	<p><span><span><span><span><span>If some aspect of a course needs urgent improvement.</span></span></span></span></span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span> <span><span><span>(e.g. Nobody responding to forum queries, cannot understand the lecturer)</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>
	</li>
</ul>

<p> </p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span>You should</span></span></span></span></span><b><span><span><span><span><span> never</span></span></span></span></span></b><span><span><span><span><span> contact any of the following people directly:</span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>- Vice Chancellor</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>- Pro-vice Chancellor Education (PVCE)</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>- Head of School</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>- CSE administrative staff</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>- CSE teaching support staff</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

<p><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>They will simply bounce the email to one of the above, thereby </span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span><span>creating an unnecessary level of indirection and a delay in the response.</span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></span></p>

      <div class="text">
        <h2>Acknowledgement of Country</h2>
        <p>UNSW respectfully acknowledges the Bidjigal, Biripai, Dharug, Gadigal, Gumbaynggirr, Ngunnawal and Wiradjuri peoples, on whose unceded lands we are privileged to learn, teach and work. We honour the Elders of these Nations, past and present, and recognise the broader Nations with whom we walk together. UNSW acknowledges the enduring connection of Aboriginal and Torres Strait Islander peoples to culture, community and Country.</p>

document.addEventListener("at-request-succeeded", function (e) {
    window.ttMETA = typeof window.ttMETA != "undefined" ? window.ttMETA : [];
    if (e && e.detail) {
        const tokens = e.detail.responseTokens;

        if (tokens) {
            tokens.forEach(function (token) {
                window.ttMETA.push({
                    CampaignName: token["activity.name"],
                    CampaignId: token["activity.id"],
                    RecipeName: token["experience.name"],
                    RecipeId: token["experience.id"],
                    OfferId: token["offer.id"],
                    OfferName: token["offer.name"],
                });
            });
        }
    }
});

</script><script>_satellite["_runScript2"](function(event, target, Promise) {
  document.addEventListener(adobe.target.event.REQUEST_SUCCEEDED, function (e) { 
    window.ttMETA= typeof(window.ttMETA)!="undefined" ? window.ttMETA : []; 
 
    var tokens=e.detail.responseTokens; 
 
    if (isEmpty(tokens)) { 
      return; 
    } 
     
    var uniqueTokens = distinct(tokens); 
 
    uniqueTokens.forEach(function(token) { 
      window.ttMETA.push({ 
        'CampaignName': token["activity.name"], 
        'CampaignId' : token["activity.id"], 
        'RecipeName': token["experience.name"], 
        'RecipeId': token["experience.id"], 
        'OfferId': token["option.id"], 
        'OfferName': token["option.name"], 
        'MboxName': e.detail.mbox}); 
      console.log(ttMETA); 
    }); 
  }); 
 
  function isEmpty(val){ 
    return (val === undefined || val == null || val.length <= 0) ? true : false; 
  } 
 
  function key(obj) { 
     return Object.keys(obj) 
    .map(function(k) { return k + "" + obj[k]; }) 
    .join(""); 
  } 
 
  function distinct(arr) { 
    var result = arr.reduce(function(acc, e) { 
      acc[key(e)] = e; 
      return acc; 
    }, {}); 
   
    return Object.keys(result) 
    .map(function(k) { return result[k]; }); 
  } 

});</script>

  var userConsent = _satellite.getVar("UserConsent");

  var consentSettings = {
      categories: {
          'adobe.ecid': false,       // Essential (e.g., Visitor ID)
          'adobe.analytics': false,  // Performance (Adobe Analytics)
          'adobe.target': false,     // Functional (Adobe Target)
          'adobe.aam': false,         // Targeting (Adobe Audience Manager)
          'adobe.adcloud': false         // Targeting (Adobe Ad Cloud)        
      }
  };

  // Map user consent to Adobe opt-in categories
  if (userConsent.categories.EssentialCookiesAllowed) {
      consentSettings.categories['adobe.ecid'] = true;
  }
  if (userConsent.categories.FunctionalCookiesAllowed) {
      consentSettings.categories['adobe.target'] = true;
  }
  if (userConsent.categories.PerformanceCookiesAllowed) {
      consentSettings.categories['adobe.analytics'] = true;
  }
  if (userConsent.categories.TargetingCookiesAllowed) {
      consentSettings.categories['adobe.aam'] = true;
      consentSettings.categories['adobe.adcloud'] = true;
  }

Event snippet for UNSW - All Pages - 00326 on https://www.unsw.edu.au/: Please do not remove.
Place this snippet on pages with events you’re tracking.
Creation date: 03/25/2020

<script>
  // Build the event payload with the current page URL for u1
  var eventPayload = {
    allow_custom_scripts: true,
    u1: window.location.href,
    u2: window.digitalData.page.formDataInfo.formID,
    send_to: 'DC-9967334/unswm0/https0+standard'
  };

  // If at least one form exists on the page, add the custom variable u2 using the form ID from digitalData
  if (
    document.forms.length > 0 && 
    window.digitalData && 
    window.digitalData.page && 
    window.digitalData.page.formDataInfo &&
    window.digitalData.page.formDataInfo.formID
  ) {
    eventPayload.u2 = window.digitalData.page.formDataInfo.formID; 
  }

  // Log the event payload in the console
  console.log("[UNSW - All Pages - 00326][DC-9967334] Firing conversion event with payload:", eventPayload);

  // Fire the conversion event on all pages
  gtag('event', 'conversion', eventPayload);
</script>
<!-- End of event snippet: Please do not remove --><script type="text/javascript">
  function showPreferenceCenter() {
      OneTrust.ToggleInfoDisplay();
  }

  function showFloatingIcon() {
    const floatyButton = document.getElementById("ot-sdk-btn-floating");
    if (floatyButton) {
      floatyButton.style.display = "block";              
    }
  }

  function insertBeforeLi(link) {
    const targetLink = document.querySelector(link);
    if (targetLink) {
      const targetLi = targetLink.closest('li');
      if (targetLi && targetLi.parentNode) {
        const newLi = document.createElement('li');
        newLi.innerHTML = '<a id="showpclink" href="#" onclick="showPreferenceCenter(); return false;">Cookie Preferences</a>';
        targetLi.parentNode.insertBefore(newLi, targetLi);
      }
    }
  }
  
  function replaceLinks() {
      if (document.getElementById("ot-sdk-btn-floating")) { 
        console.log("OneTrust is active");
        // List of possible privacy links
        const privacylinks = [
          'a[href="https://www.unsw.edu.au/privacy"][data-click_name="Privacy Statement"]',
          'a[href="https://www.unsw.edu.au/privacy"][data-click_name="Privacy statement"]',
          'a[href="https://www.unsw.edu.au/privacy"][data-click_name="Privacy Policy"]',
          'a[href="https://www.unsw.edu.au/privacy"][data-click_name="Privacy policy"]'
        ];
        replacedPrivacy = false;
        for (let i = 0; i < privacylinks.length; i++) {
          insertBeforeLi(privacylinks[i]);
          if (document.getElementById('showpclink')) {
            replacedPrivacy = true;
            break;
          }
        }
        if (!replacedPrivacy) {
          showFloatingIcon();
        }
      } 
      else {
        // Hide Cookie Preference link if OneTrust is not enabled  
        const cookieLink = document.getElementById('showpclink');
        if (cookieLink) {
          cookieLink.style.display = "none";  
        }
      }      
    }
 
    replaceLinks();
                          
</script></body></html>
